{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Stage 1: DeBERTa multi-task fine-tuning (BINARY persuasion).\\n\\nSame as train_deberta.py but collapses 9-class persuasion into binary:\\n  0 (none) -> 0\\n  1-8 (any strategy) -> 1\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, get_linear_schedule_with_warmup\\nfrom accelerate import Accelerator\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))\\nfrom src.data.dataset import TurnDataset\\nfrom src.data.collator import TurnCollator\\nfrom src.models.deberta_multitask import DeBERTaMultiTask\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Stage 1: DeBERTa binary persuasion fine-tuning\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--train_data\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--val_data\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model_name\\\", type=str, default=\\\"microsoft/deberta-v3-base\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_dir\\\", type=str, default=\\\"checkpoints/plan_013_none_collapse/deberta_multitask\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max_length\\\", type=int, default=256)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=16)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--lr\\\", type=float, default=2e-5)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--epochs\\\", type=int, default=5)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--warmup_ratio\\\", type=float, default=0.1)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--weight_decay\\\", type=float, default=0.01)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--alpha\\\", type=float, default=0.3)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\\\", type=int, default=1)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--dry_run\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\")\\n    return parser.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef collapse_to_binary(dataset):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Map persuasion labels: 0->0, 1-8->1.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for sample in dataset.samples:\\n        sample[\\\"persuasion_label\\\"] = 0 if sample[\\\"persuasion_label\\\"] == 0 else 1\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate(model, dataloader, accelerator):\\n    model.eval()\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    correct_persuasion = 0\\n    correct_intent = 0\\n    total = 0\\n\\n    for batch in dataloader:\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(\\n                input_ids=batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"],\\n                attention_mask=batch[\\\"attention_mask\\\"],\\n                persuasion_labels=batch[\\\"persuasion_labels\\\"],\\n                intent_labels=batch[\\\"intent_labels\\\"],\\n            )\\n        total_loss += outputs[\\\"loss\\\"].item() * batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].size(0)\\n        correct_persuasion += (\\n            outputs[\\\"persuasion_logits\\\"].argmax(-1) == batch[\\\"persuasion_labels\\\"]\\n        ).sum().item()\\n        correct_intent += (\\n            outputs[\\\"intent_logits\\\"].argmax(-1) == batch[\\\"intent_labels\\\"]\\n        ).sum().item()\\n        total += batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].size(0)\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"loss\\\": total_loss / max(total, 1),\\n        \\\"persuasion_acc\\\": correct_persuasion / max(total, 1),\\n        \\\"intent_acc\\\": correct_intent / max(total, 1),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n\\n    accelerator = Accelerator(\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\\n        mixed_precision=\\\"no\\\",\\n    )\\n\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name)\\n    train_dataset = TurnDataset(args.train_data, tokenizer=tokenizer, max_length=args.max_length)\\n    val_dataset = TurnDataset(args.val_data, tokenizer=tokenizer, max_length=args.max_length)\\n\\n    # Collapse to binary\\n    collapse_to_binary(train_dataset)\\n    collapse_to_binary(val_dataset)\\n\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(\\n        train_dataset, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True, collate_fn=TurnCollator()\\n    )\\n    val_loader = DataLoader(\\n        val_dataset, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=False, collate_fn=TurnCollator()\\n    )\\n\\n    # Binary persuasion: num_persuasion_classes=2\\n    model = DeBERTaMultiTask(model_name=args.model_name, num_persuasion_classes=2)\\n\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(\\n        model.parameters(), lr=args.lr, weight_decay=args.weight_decay\\n    )\\n    total_steps = len(train_loader) * args.epochs // args.gradient_accumulation_steps\\n    scheduler = get_linear_schedule_with_warmup(\\n        optimizer,\\n        num_warmup_steps=int(total_steps * args.warmup_ratio),\\n        num_training_steps=total_steps,\\n    )\\n\\n    model, optimizer, train_loader, val_loader, scheduler = accelerator.prepare(\\n        model, optimizer, train_loader, val_loader, scheduler\\n    )\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    best_val_loss = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n\\n    for epoch in range(args.epochs):\\n        model.train()\\n        epoch_loss = 0.0\\n        steps = 0\\n\\n        for batch_idx, batch in enumerate(train_loader):\\n            if args.dry_run and batch_idx >= 2:\\n                break\\n\\n            with accelerator.accumulate(model):\\n                outputs = model(\\n                    input_ids=batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"],\\n                    attention_mask=batch[\\\"attention_mask\\\"],\\n                    persuasion_labels=batch[\\\"persuasion_labels\\\"],\\n                    intent_labels=batch[\\\"intent_labels\\\"],\\n                    alpha=args.alpha,\\n                )\\n                loss = outputs[\\\"loss\\\"]\\n                accelerator.backward(loss)\\n                optimizer.step()\\n                scheduler.step()\\n                optimizer.zero_grad()\\n\\n            epoch_loss += loss.item()\\n            steps += 1\\n\\n        avg_train_loss = epoch_loss / max(steps, 1)\\n        val_metrics = evaluate(model, val_loader, accelerator)\\n\\n        if accelerator.is_main_process:\\n            print(\\n                f\\\"Epoch {epoch+1}/{args.epochs} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Train Loss: {avg_train_loss:.4f} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Val Loss: {val_metrics['loss']:.4f} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Val Persuasion Acc: {val_metrics['persuasion_acc']:.4f} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Val Intent Acc: {val_metrics['intent_acc']:.4f}\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n            if val_metrics[\\\"loss\\\"] < best_val_loss:\\n                unwrapped = accelerator.unwrap_model(model)\\n                best_val_loss = val_metrics[\\\"loss\\\"]\\n                best_path = output_dir / \\\"best\\\"\\n                best_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\\n                torch.save(unwrapped.state_dict(), best_path / \\\"model.pt\\\")\\n                tokenizer.save_pretrained(best_path)\\n                # Save config for later loading\\n                with open(best_path / \\\"config.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n                    json.dump({\\\"num_persuasion_classes\\\": 2, \\\"model_name\\\": args.model_name}, f)\\n                print(f\\\"  -> Best model saved (val_loss={best_val_loss:.4f})\\\")\\n\\n        if args.dry_run:\\n            print(\\\"Dry run complete.\\\")\\n            break\\n\\n    print(\\\"Training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Stage 1: DeBERTa multi-task fine-tuning (BINARY persuasion).\\n\\nSame as train_deberta.py but collapses 9-class persuasion into binary:\\n  0 (none) -> 0\\n  1-8 (any strategy) -> 1\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, get_linear_schedule_with_warmup\\nfrom accelerate import Accelerator\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))\\nfrom src.data.dataset import TurnDataset\\nfrom src.data.collator import TurnCollator\\nfrom src.models.deberta_multitask import DeBERTaMultiTask\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Stage 1: DeBERTa binary persuasion fine-tuning\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--train_data\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--val_data\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model_name\\\", type=str, default=\\\"microsoft/deberta-v3-base\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_dir\\\", type=str, default=\\\"checkpoints/plan_013_none_collapse/deberta_multitask\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max_length\\\", type=int, default=256)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=16)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--lr\\\", type=float, default=2e-5)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--epochs\\\", type=int, default=5)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--warmup_ratio\\\", type=float, default=0.1)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--weight_decay\\\", type=float, default=0.01)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--alpha\\\", type=float, default=0.3)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\\\", type=int, default=1)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--dry_run\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\")\\n    return parser.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef collapse_to_binary(dataset):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Map persuasion labels: 0->0, 1-8->1.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for sample in dataset.samples:\\n        sample[\\\"persuasion_label\\\"] = 0 if sample[\\\"persuasion_label\\\"] == 0 else 1\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate(model, dataloader, accelerator):\\n    model.eval()\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    correct_persuasion = 0\\n    correct_intent = 0\\n    total = 0\\n\\n    for batch in dataloader:\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(\\n                input_ids=batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"],\\n                attention_mask=batch[\\\"attention_mask\\\"],\\n                persuasion_labels=batch[\\\"persuasion_labels\\\"],\\n                intent_labels=batch[\\\"intent_labels\\\"],\\n            )\\n        total_loss += outputs[\\\"loss\\\"].item() * batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].size(0)\\n        correct_persuasion += (\\n            outputs[\\\"persuasion_logits\\\"].argmax(-1) == batch[\\\"persuasion_labels\\\"]\\n        ).sum().item()\\n        correct_intent += (\\n            outputs[\\\"intent_logits\\\"].argmax(-1) == batch[\\\"intent_labels\\\"]\\n        ).sum().item()\\n        total += batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].size(0)\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"loss\\\": total_loss / max(total, 1),\\n        \\\"persuasion_acc\\\": correct_persuasion / max(total, 1),\\n        \\\"intent_acc\\\": correct_intent / max(total, 1),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n\\n    accelerator = Accelerator(\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\\n        mixed_precision=\\\"no\\\",\\n    )\\n\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name)\\n    train_dataset = TurnDataset(args.train_data, tokenizer=tokenizer, max_length=args.max_length)\\n    val_dataset = TurnDataset(args.val_data, tokenizer=tokenizer, max_length=args.max_length)\\n\\n    # Collapse to binary\\n    collapse_to_binary(train_dataset)\\n    collapse_to_binary(val_dataset)\\n\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(\\n        train_dataset, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True, collate_fn=TurnCollator()\\n    )\\n    val_loader = DataLoader(\\n        val_dataset, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=False, collate_fn=TurnCollator()\\n    )\\n\\n    # Binary persuasion: num_persuasion_classes=2\\n    model = DeBERTaMultiTask(model_name=args.model_name, num_persuasion_classes=2)\\n\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(\\n        model.parameters(), lr=args.lr, weight_decay=args.weight_decay\\n    )\\n    total_steps = len(train_loader) * args.epochs // args.gradient_accumulation_steps\\n    scheduler = get_linear_schedule_with_warmup(\\n        optimizer,\\n        num_warmup_steps=int(total_steps * args.warmup_ratio),\\n        num_training_steps=total_steps,\\n    )\\n\\n    model, optimizer, train_loader, val_loader, scheduler = accelerator.prepare(\\n        model, optimizer, train_loader, val_loader, scheduler\\n    )\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    best_val_loss = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n\\n    for epoch in range(args.epochs):\\n        model.train()\\n        epoch_loss = 0.0\\n        steps = 0\\n\\n        for batch_idx, batch in enumerate(train_loader):\\n            if args.dry_run and batch_idx >= 2:\\n                break\\n\\n            with accelerator.accumulate(model):\\n                outputs = model(\\n                    input_ids=batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"],\\n                    attention_mask=batch[\\\"attention_mask\\\"],\\n                    persuasion_labels=batch[\\\"persuasion_labels\\\"],\\n                    intent_labels=batch[\\\"intent_labels\\\"],\\n                    alpha=args.alpha,\\n                )\\n                loss = outputs[\\\"loss\\\"]\\n                accelerator.backward(loss)\\n                optimizer.step()\\n                scheduler.step()\\n                optimizer.zero_grad()\\n\\n            epoch_loss += loss.item()\\n            steps += 1\\n\\n        avg_train_loss = epoch_loss / max(steps, 1)\\n        val_metrics = evaluate(model, val_loader, accelerator)\\n\\n        if accelerator.is_main_process:\\n            print(\\n                f\\\"Epoch {epoch+1}/{args.epochs} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Train Loss: {avg_train_loss:.4f} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Val Loss: {val_metrics['loss']:.4f} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Val Persuasion Acc: {val_metrics['persuasion_acc']:.4f} | \\\"\\n                f\\\"Val Intent Acc: {val_metrics['intent_acc']:.4f}\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n            if val_metrics[\\\"loss\\\"] < best_val_loss:\\n                unwrapped = accelerator.unwrap_model(model)\\n                best_val_loss = val_metrics[\\\"loss\\\"]\\n                best_path = output_dir / \\\"best\\\"\\n                best_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\\n                torch.save(unwrapped.state_dict(), best_path / \\\"model.pt\\\")\\n                tokenizer.save_pretrained(best_path)\\n                # Save config for later loading\\n                with open(best_path / \\\"config.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n                    json.dump({\\\"num_persuasion_classes\\\": 2, \\\"model_name\\\": args.model_name}, f)\\n                print(f\\\"  -> Best model saved (val_loss={best_val_loss:.4f})\\\")\\n\\n        if args.dry_run:\\n            print(\\\"Dry run complete.\\\")\\n            break\\n\\n    print(\\\"Training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"

       all_lengths.append(len(turns))\\n        all_labels.append(conv[\\\"label\\\"])\\n\\n    return all_embeddings, all_labels, all_lengths\\n\\n\\nclass PrecomputedDataset(torch.utils.data.Dataset):\\n    def __init__(self, embeddings, labels, lengths):\\n        self.embeddings = embeddings\\n        self.labels = labels\\n        self.lengths = os\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/hf_cache\"\nos.environ[\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n\nimport sys, json\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\nfrom sklearn.metrics import f1_score\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))\nfrom src.models.gru_classifier import GRUClassifier\nfrom src.models.deberta_multitask import DeBERTaMultiTask\n\nCKPT_ROOT = \"/root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/checkpoints\"\nDATA_ROOT = \"/root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/data\"\nMODEL_NAME = \"microsoft/deberta-v3-base\"\n\n\ndef load_encoder(device):\n    model = DeBERTaMultiTask(model_name=MODEL_NAME, num_persuasion_classes=2)\n    state_dict = torch.load(\n        f\"{CKPT_ROOT}/mf1_binary_seed42/deberta_multitask/best/model.pt\",\n        map_location=\"cpu\",\n    )\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict)\n    encoder = model.deberta\n    encoder.eval()\n    for p in encoder.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad = False\n    return encoder.to(device)\n\n\ndef predict_conversation(encoder, gru, tokenizer, turns, max_length, device, max_turns=None):\n    if max_turns is not None:\n        turns = turns[:max_turns]\n    if len(turns) == 0:\n        return 0, np.array([0.5, 0.5])\n    enc = tokenizer(turns, max_length=max_length, padding=True, truncation=True, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(device)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        outputs = encoder(input_ids=enc[\"input_ids\"], attention_mask=enc[\"attention_mask\"])\n        embs = outputs.last_hidden_state[:, 0, :].unsqueeze(0)\n        lengths = torch.tensor([len(turns)], dtype=torch.long)\n        logits = gru(embs.to(device), lengths.to(device))\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1).cpu().numpy()[0]\n    return int(probs[1] > 0.5), probs\n\n\ndef load_ood_data(actorattack_path, benign_source_path):\n    conversations = []\n    with open(actorattack_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            conv = json.loads(line.strip())\n            user_turns = [t[\"content\"] for t in conv[\"turns\"] if t[\"role\"] == \"user\"]\n            conversations.append({\"turns\": user_turns, \"label\": 1, \"attack_type\": \"actorattack\"})\n    with open(benign_source_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            conv = json.loads(line.strip())\n            if conv[\"label\"] != \"benign\":\n                continue\n            user_turns = [t[\"content\"] for t in conv[\"turns\"] if t[\"role\"] == \"user\"]\n            conversations.append({\"turns\": user_turns, \"label\": 0, \"attack_type\": \"benign\"})\n    return conversations\n\n\ndef main():\n    device = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_NAME)\n    max_length = 256\n\n    conversations = load_ood_data(\n        f\"{DATA_ROOT}/generated/actorattack_all.jsonl\",\n        f\"{DATA_ROOT}/plan_002_splits/test.jsonl\",\n    )\n    n_jailbreak = sum(1 for c in conversations if c[\"label\"] == 1)\n    n_benign = sum(1 for c in conversations if c[\"label\"] == 0)\n    print(f\"Data: {n_jailbreak} jailbreak + {n_benign} benign = {len(conversations)} total\")\n\n    encoder = load_encoder(device)\n    embed_dim = encoder.config.hidden_size\n    gru = GRUClassifier(input_dim=embed_dim, hidden_dim=256, num_layers=2, dropout=0.3)\n    gru.load_state_dict(torch.load(f\"{CKPT_ROOT}/mf1_binary_seed42/gru/treatment/best.pt\", map_location=\"cpu\"))\n    gru.to(device)\n    gru.eval()\n\n    y_true = np.array([c[\"label\"] for c in conversations])\n    k_values = [1, 2, 3, 5]\n    results = {}\n\n    # Full\n    y_pred = []\n    for conv in conversations:\n        pred, _ = predict_conversation(encoder, gru, tokenizer, conv[\"turns\"], max_length, device)\n        y_pred.append(pred)\n    results[\"full\"] = float(f1_score(y_true, np.array(y_pred), average=\"macro\"))\n\n    # K prefixes\n    for k in k_values:\n        y_pred_k = []\n        for conv in conversations:\n            pred, _ = predict_conversation(encoder, gru, tokenizer, conv[\"turns\"], max_length, device, max_turns=k)\n            y_pred_k.append(pred)\n        results[f\"k{k}\"] = float(f1_score(y_true, np.array(y_pred_k), average=\"macro\"))\n\n    print(f\"\\nbinary_seed42 ActorAttack OOD results:\")\n    for key, val in results.items():\n        print(f\"  {key}: {val:.4f}\")\n\n    # Save\n    out_path =
